main07\060_partnernoomoi\partnernoomoi.lua.txt





`Partner Name`: ... ... ...

`Partner Name`: Hey, `Hero Name`... 
You still awake?

`Partner Name`: I just can't seem to sleep in this humidity...


`Partner Name`: `Hero Name`...

`Partner Name`: You know, when I was watching Leavanny
and the others today, it made me think...

`Partner Name`: You've got friends and family, too, right?
In your world, `Hero Name`. The human world.

`Partner Name`: How...do you think they're feeling now?

`Partner Name`: Are you all close?

`Partner Name`: ... ... ... I...

`Partner Name`: I don't... have anyone like that. 
Parents... Brothers or sisters...


`Hero Name`: (... ...)


`Partner Name`: I've been alone as long as I can remember.

`Partner Name`: I never had any friends either... 
I wanted them, but I couldn't make any.

`Partner Name`: The relationships between Pokémon just
aren't very good in this world...

`Partner Name`: If we could just get along a little better...
But everyone's so quick to pick fights.

`Partner Name`: Even Pokémon who seem like friends on
the surface really aren't...

`Partner Name`: No one says what they mean. It's all just
lies and lip service...

`Partner Name`: I just can't live like that. That's why
I've never had any friends up until now.

`Partner Name`: I was probably no different from Virizion,
now that I think about it...


`Hero Name`: (... ... ...)


`Partner Name`: But, you know...

`Partner Name`: At least I never stopped looking.

`Partner Name`: Not just for some superficial alliance...

`Partner Name`: ...but for someone I could trust with the
real me. A true friend.

`Partner Name`: I thought how great it'd be to actually do
something real with that kind of friend.

`Partner Name`: Like making this kind of paradise...

`Partner Name`: And that's why... every day here is really
so much fun for me.

`Partner Name`: Thank you, `Hero Name`.

`Partner Name`: I hope... these days will continue...

`Partner Name`: ... I think I can finally fall asleep now.

`Partner Name`: Guess you already did, huh, `Hero Name`...

`Partner Name`: I'm gonna go to sleep, too. 
See you tomorrow, `Hero Name`.

`Partner Name`: Good night.

`Virizion Sad`
`Virizion Name`: I think everyone just knows that things
don't always work out in the real world.
`Virizion Name`: No matter how hard you try, things can't
always be fixed...
`Virizion Name`: And the simple act of trying sometimes
makes things far worse...
`Virizion Name`: I think everyone has had such experiences.
`Virizion Name`: And when you experience that kind of
letdown again and again...
`Virizion Name`: ...then after a while, whenever anything
happens, you find yourself thinking: 
`Virizion Name`: "Trying just makes everything hurt worse."
`Virizion Name`: "What's the point of trying, then?"
`Virizion Name`: Deciding from the very start that there's
nothing you can do is easier. It hurts less.
`Virizion Name`: All those experiences of failure
and disappointment...
`Virizion Name`: ...they put the brakes on any positive
thought you might have.
`Virizion Name`: Negativity gets so deep into your heart,
and it's difficult to get it out again...

something that's so ingrained in someone...

`Emolga Surprised`
`Emolga Name`: We really got here without having to go
through a single dungeon! That's crazy!
`Emolga Name`: And we even came across the sky!

`Emolga Name`: What is with these Entercards, huh?!
They're unbelievable!

	Espeon: *Face Normal`
	Espeon: They are indeed impressive.
	Espeon: To be able to make cards like this...
Well, if I'm being honest... I'm quite jealous.

	Umbreon: *Face Normal`
	Umbreon: Well, we are dungeon researchers, after
all. So I know how you feel, but...
	Umbreon: This is no time for that kind of chitchat.
	Umbreon: We made it to the Glacier Palace.
That's what matters.
	Umbreon: Now we need to get to the Bittercold... 
and destroy it!
	Umbreon: We need to keep our focus on that and
move forward!

💦

`Emolga Name`: W...wait a sec!

`Emolga Special2`
`Emolga Name`: It's great that we made it here, of course,
but, um... what about getting back?
`Emolga Name`: How do we get back to Paradise? 
We're way up here in the sky...

	Umbreon: *Face Normal`
	Umbreon: Don't worry.
	Umbreon: We should be able to get back again by
using Keldeo's Entercards.

`Emolga Special2`
`Emolga Name`: O-oh... Really?

💦
`Emolga RELIEF*
`Emolga Name`: Well, that's OK then. Phew.

`Emolga Special3`
`Emolga Name`: I was afraid for a second that maybe you
guys weren't planning on making it back...

	Espeon: *Face Normal`
	Espeon: That's ridiculous.
	Espeon: We are absolutely going to return
to Paradise safe and sound.

`Virizion Normal`
`Virizion Name`: After all, if we didn't, how would we ever
be able to face Dunsparce again?

💦
`Emolga Special3`
`Emolga Name`: Haha... Yeah, I guess that's true...

`Emolga Special1`
`Emolga Name`: All right! Then let's go do this thing!

`Virizion Normal`
`Virizion Name`: Salamence came from up here. 
So that means...
`Virizion Name`: We should assume Munna and the
others are also up here somewhere.


`Partner Think`

`Partner Name`: Do you think they noticed our arrival?

	Umbreon: *Face Normal`
	Umbreon: Hard to say.

	Umbreon: But if they haven't, we should use that
to our advantage.

	Espeon: *Face Normal`
	Espeon: We'll draw attention if we all travel
together in one big group, though.
	Espeon: If we split up, we should be able to avoid
getting caught by Munna.
	Espeon: And if her thugs do find us and chase us,
at least they'll have to split their forces.

	Umbreon: *Face Normal`
	Umbreon: Sounds fair. Let's split into three groups.
	Umbreon: Espeon and I will stick together. 
Virizion and Emolga should team up...
	Umbreon: And finally `Hero Name` and `Partner Name`.
	Umbreon: Each of our groups will go a different way.
Does that sound all right to everyone?


`Partner Normal`

`Partner Name`: Fine with me.

`Virizion Normal`
`Virizion Name`: Stay safe, everyone...

`Emolga Normal`
`Emolga Name`: Make sure you don't lose, OK?

	Umbreon: *Face Normal`

	Espeon: *Face Normal`

`Virizion Normal`
`Virizion Name`: We should head out, too.

`Emolga Normal`
`Emolga Name`: Yeah.

✨
`Emolga Normal`
`Emolga Name`: Be careful, `Hero Name` and `Partner Name`!


`Partner Normal`

`Partner Name`: You all be careful, too!


`Partner Normal`

main17\050_burakkiitoeefi\burakkiitoeefi.lua.txt
	Umbreon: *Face Normal`
	Umbreon: Looks like we've still got a long way to go.

	Espeon: *Face Think`
	Espeon: ... Umbreon...

	Umbreon: *Face Normal`
	Umbreon: What is it?

	Espeon: *Face Think`
	Espeon: When we... When Emolga asked you about
getting back...
	Espeon: Why did you tell him...
	Espeon: Why did you pretend we could use Keldeo's
Entercards to get back? Why did you lie?

	Umbreon: *Face Normal`
	Umbreon: You played along, too, you know.
Why are you questioning it now?

	Espeon: *Face Normal`
	Espeon: Well, it wasn't just me. Virizion did, too.
	Espeon: I just didn't want to make Emolga and the
others too afraid of what was coming.

	Umbreon: *Face Normal`
	Umbreon: Me too. There's your answer. Anyway, we
don't know for sure that it was a lie.
	Umbreon: We just can't verify it. If we had the
time to study them...
	Umbreon: Maybe we could find a way to use
those Entercards to get back home.
	Umbreon: But that's just not in the cards for us.
Right now we have to find a way to fight
the Bittercold.
	Umbreon: If the world ends, there won't be anything
for anyone to return to anyway.

	Espeon: *Face Sad`
	Espeon: ... Umbreon...
	Espeon: I don't know if it's even possible, but...

	Espeon: *Face Tearyeyed`
	Espeon: I still want us to make it back.
	Espeon: You, me, everyone else...
We all have to make it back.
Back to Paradise.

	Umbreon: *Face Normal`
	Umbreon: I know, Espeon.

way at first...

Keldeo and all...

chance I got...

excuse for a Pokémon.

my friend.
